# DelugeRPG — Consolidated Technical & Gameplay Reference

> Combined reference built from Batch 1 (archives 1–20) and Batch 2 (archives 21–26). Source-derived; not independently verified against the live game.

## Scope and integrity

- Input archives: **26** (`1.zip`–`26.zip`).
- Captured page entries: **26**; unique URL paths: **21**.
- Processed file entries excluding per-archive metadata/runtime files: **6,333**.
- Exact-byte duplicate entries removed: **4,952**; unique retained file contents: **1,381**.
- Unique retained payload size: **41.7 MB** before ZIP compression.
- Different versions of same-named files are retained; only byte-identical files are deduplicated.
- Authentication/session runtime snapshots (`_globals.js`, `_storage.json`) are excluded; browser global names/types are indexed without values. API logs retain URL paths and query parameter names only, not query values.
- Page HTML copies remove scripts/iframes, hidden token fields and inline event handlers; scripts and assets are preserved separately.

                    <div class="infoexp"><b>Exp:</b> ${b.exp}${typeof g.show_experaser!=="undefined"?` <i class="fad fa-eraser experaser" data-exp="${b.exp.replace(",","")}"></i>`:""}</div>
                    <div class="itype">${poketypes(b)}</div>

                        <div>${b.attack1}</div>
                        <div>${b.attack2}</div>

                    </div>`:typeof b.attack!=="undefined"&&typeof b.attack.a1!=="undefined"&&typeof b.attack.a1.name!=="undefined"&&(e+=`<div class="attk">

                        <div class="hoverclr-${toLower(b.attack.a2.type)}">${b.attack.a2.name}</div>
                        <div class="hoverclr-${toLower(b.attack.a3.type)}">${b.attack.a3.name}</div>
                        <div class="hoverclr-${toLower(b.attack.a4.type)}">${b.attack.a4.name}</div>

function confirmer(){$(".opts > .remove").length&&$(".opts > .remove").off("click").confirm({text:'<div style="font-size:16px;text-align:center;margin:30px auto;">Are you sure?</div>',title:"Confirmation",confirm:function(a){var c=a.parents(".row").attr("id");c=c.slice(4);a=extractAjaxLink(a);a=a.slice(a.indexOf("/tok/")+5);$.get("/ajax/trade/remove/"+c+"/tok/"+a,function(b){if(b.status=="invtoken"){b="Error Occurred. Refresh page and try again.";var d="danger"}else b.status=="nopoke"?(b="No Such Pokemon.",
d="danger"):b.status=="dberr"?(b="Database Error Occurred.",d="danger"):(b="<b>"+b.poke+"</b> was successfully removed from trade.",d="success",$("#poke"+c).slideUp("slow",function(){$(this).remove()}));b&&alertBox.show(b,{type:d,align:"center",expireTime:d=="danger"?15E3:5E3})},"json").fail(function(){alertBox.show("Error removing from trade. Please try again.",{type:"danger",align:"center"})});return!1},confirmButton:"Yes I am",cancelButton:"No",cancelButtonClass:"btn-danger"});$(".yopt > .offercancel").length&&
$(".yopt > .offercancel").off("click").confirm({text:'<div style="font-size:16px;text-align:center;margin:30px auto;">Are you sure?</div>',title:"Confirmation",confirm:function(a){var c=a.attr("id");c=c.slice(4);a=a.data("href");a=a.slice(a.indexOf("/tok/")+5);$.post("/ajax/trade/cancel",{id:c,tok:a},function(b){if(b.status=="invtoken"){b='<div class="notify_error"> Error Occurred. Refresh the page and try again.</div>';var d="danger"}else b.status=="error"?(b='<div class="notify_error"> No such offer exists on the database.</div>',
d="danger"):b.status=="dberr"?(b='<div class="notify_error"> Database Error Occurred.</div>',d="danger"):(b='<div class="notify_done" id="removesuccess">The trade was successfully cancelled.</div>',d="success",$("#trd_"+c).parents(".yoffers").slideUp("slow",function(){$(this).remove()}));Modal.hide();alertBox.show(b,{type:d,align:"center",expireTime:d=="danger"?15E3:5E3})},"json").fail(function(){Modal.hide();alertBox.show("Error cancelling trade. Please try again.",{type:"danger",align:"center"})});
return!1},confirmButton:"Yes I am",cancelButton:"No",cancelButtonClass:"btn-danger"})};

## Guidance for AI assistants
- Treat the captured files as evidence only for behavior present in these captures; do not assume current live behavior is identical.
- Inspect actual source files, not only this master index. The complete retained files are under `source/` and `assets/`.
- Do not invent missing functions, endpoints, parameters, mechanics or server-side behavior.
- When explaining a feature, cite its page capture, source file, and function where possible; distinguish direct observation from inference.
- Client-side code and network paths do not establish full server-side implementation. Server source is not included.
- Repeated URLs may represent different captures at different times. Preserve meaningful differences; do not discard non-identical versions.
- This is Batch 1 + Batch 2 only. Incorporate future batches into this same reference before claiming coverage is complete.
